local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
if not LP then
	Players:GetPropertyChangedSignal("LocalPlayer"):Wait()
	LP = Players.LocalPlayer
end
if not LP then
	warn("[AmbitiousHub] no LocalPlayer")
	return
end
local pg = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") or LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)
if not pg then
	warn("[AmbitiousHub] no PlayerGui")
	return
end

local function parentGui(gui)
	if not gui then return false end
	local function tryParent(target)
		if not target then return false end
		local ok = pcall(function() gui.Parent = target end)
		return ok and gui.Parent ~= nil
	end
	local hui
	pcall(function()
		if typeof(gethui) == "function" then hui = gethui() end
	end)
	if tryParent(hui) then return true end
	local cg
	pcall(function() cg = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
	if tryParent(cg) then return true end
	if tryParent(pg) then return true end
	return false
end

pcall(function()
	for _, g in ipairs({pg}) do
		local old = g:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHub")
		if old then old:Destroy() end
	end
	pcall(function()
		local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
		local old = cg:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHub")
		if old then old:Destroy() end
	end)
	pcall(function()
		if typeof(gethui)=="function" then
			local h = gethui()
			if h then
				local old = h:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHub")
				if old then old:Destroy() end
			end
		end
	end)
end)

                    UIGradient357.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0.55,0),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,0.1,0),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,0.55,0)})
                    UIGradient357.Parent = UIStroke336

                TabPad4 = Instance.new("UIPadding")
                TabPad4.Name = "TabPad"
                TabPad4.Parent = VISUALS2

            SETTINGS3 = Instance.new("TextButton")
            SETTINGS3.Name = "SETTINGS"

            SETTINGS3.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(5,5,8)
            SETTINGS3.BackgroundTransparency = 0.72
            SETTINGS3.BorderSizePixel = 0
            SETTINGS3.Text = "SETTINGS"
            SETTINGS3.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170,170,180)
            SETTINGS3.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            SETTINGS3.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.35
            SETTINGS3.AutoButtonColor = false
            SETTINGS3.Parent = Tabs

                UICorner456 = Instance.new("UICorner")
                UICorner456.Name = "UICorner"
                UICorner456.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,7)
                UICorner456.Parent = SETTINGS3

                UIStroke337 = Instance.new("UIStroke")

                UIStroke337.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90,90,105)
                UIStroke337.Transparency = 0.52
                UIStroke337.Parent = SETTINGS3

                    UIGradient358 = Instance.new("UIGradient")
                    UIGradient358.Name = "UIGradient"
                    UIGradient358.Color = ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,Color3.fromRGB(155,160,185)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255))})
                    UIGradient358.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0.55,0),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,0.1,0),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,0.55,0)})
                    UIGradient358.Parent = UIStroke337

                TabPad5 = Instance.new("UIPadding")
                TabPad5.Name = "TabPad"
                TabPad5.Parent = SETTINGS3

        AmbitiousMainScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
        AmbitiousMainScale.Name = "AmbitiousMainScale"
        AmbitiousMainScale.Scale = 0.75
        AmbitiousMainScale.Parent = Main

    MiniFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
    MiniFrame.Name = "MiniFrame"

        UICorner457 = Instance.new("UICorner")

        UIStroke338 = Instance.new("UIStroke")

if not _ok then
	warn("[AmbitiousHub] BUILD ERROR: ", tostring(_err))
	print("[AmbitiousHub] BUILD ERROR: ", tostring(_err))
	return
end
print("[AmbitiousHub] build ok, parent:", AmbitiousHub and AmbitiousHub.Parent and AmbitiousHub.Parent.Name)

task.defer(function()
	if not AmbitiousHub or not AmbitiousHub.Parent then
		warn("[AmbitiousHub] hub not parented")
		return
	end
	AmbitiousHub.Enabled = true
	local main = AmbitiousHub:FindFirstChild("Main")
	if not main then
		warn("[AmbitiousHub] Main missing")
		return
	end
	local TS = game:GetService("TweenService")
	local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
	local uiLocked = false
	local toggleState = {}

	local closeBtn = main:FindFirstChild("Close")
	local mini = AmbitiousHub:FindFirstChild("MiniFrame")
	if closeBtn then
		closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			main.Visible = false
			if mini then mini.Visible = true end
		end)
	end
	if mini then
		mini.Visible = false
		local mb = mini:FindFirstChild("MiniButton") or mini:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextButton", true)
		if mb then
			mb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				main.Visible = true
				mini.Visible = false
			end)
		end
		local dragging, start, startPos
		mini.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if uiLocked then return end
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				dragging, start, startPos = true, input.Position, mini.Position
			end
		end)
		UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				dragging = false
			end
		end)
		UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
			if not dragging or uiLocked then return end
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				local d = input.Position - start
				mini.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
			end
		end)
	end

	local lockBtn = main:FindFirstChild("LockGUI")
	if lockBtn then
		lockBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			uiLocked = not uiLocked
			lockBtn.Text = uiLocked and "LOCKED" or "LOCK"
			lockBtn.TextColor3 = uiLocked and Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 80) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		end)
	end

	do
		local dragging, start, startPos
		main.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if uiLocked then return end
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				dragging, start, startPos = true, input.Position, main.Position
			end
		end)
		UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				dragging = false
			end
		end)
		UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
			if not dragging or uiLocked then return end
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				local d = input.Position - start
				main.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
			end
		end)
	end

	local tabs = main:FindFirstChild("Tabs")
	local content = main:FindFirstChild("Content")
	if tabs and content then
		local pages = {}
		for _, child in ipairs(content:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("ScrollingFrame") or (child:IsA("Frame") and child.Name ~= "UIListLayout") then
				pages[child.Name] = child
			end
		end
		local function showTab(name)
			for n, page in pairs(pages) do
				page.Visible = (n == name)
			end
			for _, btn in ipairs(tabs:GetChildren()) do
				if btn:IsA("TextButton") then
					local on = btn.Name == name
					btn.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.28 or 0.72
					btn.TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255) or Color3.fromRGB(170,170,180)
					local st = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
					if st then
						st.Transparency = on and 0.05 or 0.52
						st.Color = on and Color3.fromRGB(245,245,255) or Color3.fromRGB(90,90,105)
					end
				end
			end
		end
		for _, btn in ipairs(tabs:GetChildren()) do
			if btn:IsA("TextButton") then
				btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					showTab(btn.Name)
				end)
			end
		end
		local first = tabs:FindFirstChild("MOVEMENT") or tabs:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextButton")
		if first then showTab(first.Name) end
	end

	local function setToggleVisual(btn, on)
		local track = btn:FindFirstChild("Track")
		if not track then return end
		local fill = track:FindFirstChild("Fill")
		local knob = track:FindFirstChild("Knob")
		if fill then fill.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1 end
		if knob then
			knob.Position = on and UDim2.new(1, -19, 0.5, -8) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -8)
		end
		local row = btn.Parent
		if row then
			local stroke = row:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
			if stroke then
				stroke.Color = on and Color3.fromRGB(216,180,254) or Color3.fromRGB(60,60,72)
				stroke.Transparency = on and 0.15 or 0.38
			end
		end
	end

	local count = 0
	for _, d in ipairs(main:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("TextButton") and d.Name == "ToggleButton" then
			count = count + 1
			local id = d:GetFullName()
			toggleState[id] = false
			setToggleVisual(d, false)
			d.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				toggleState[id] = not toggleState[id]
				setToggleVisual(d, toggleState[id])
			end)
		end
	end
	print("[AmbitiousHub] wired toggles:", count)
end)
